// Enhanced Nexus Engine with robust Ollama integration
import axios, { AxiosError, AxiosResponse } from 'axios';
import { config } from '../config';

// Types
interface OllamaOptions {
  temperature?: number;
  num_predict?: number;
  top_p?: number;
  presence_penalty?: number;
  frequency_penalty?: number;
  stop?: string[];
  [key: string]: any;
}

interface OllamaRequest {
  model: string;
  prompt: string;
  stream?: boolean;
  options?: OllamaOptions;
  raw?: boolean;
}

interface OllamaResponse {
  model: string;
  response: string;
  done: boolean;
  context?: number[];
  total_duration?: number;
  load_duration?: number;
  prompt_eval_count?: number;
  eval_count?: number;
}

interface AgentResponse {
  agent: string;
  decision: string;
  reasoning: string;
  confidence: number;
  rawResponse: string;
  streaming: boolean;
  chunks: string[];
}

interface ModelMetadata {
  name: string;
  digest: string;
  details: {
    format?: string;
    family?: string;
    parameter_size?: string;
    quantization?: string;
    modified_at?: string;
    size?: number;
  };
}

// Enhanced Ollama Bridge with streaming support
export class OllamaBridge {
  private static instance: OllamaBridge;
  private models: ModelMetadata[] = [];
  private lastModelUpdate = 0;
  
  static getInstance(): OllamaBridge {
    if (!OllamaBridge.instance) {
      OllamaBridge.instance = new OllamaBridge();
    }
    return OllamaBridge.instance;
  }
  
  // Enhanced query with streaming support
  async queryOllama(
    prompt: string,
    model: string = 'llama3',
    options: OllamaOptions = {},
    stream: boolean = false
  ): Promise<OllamaResponse | ReadableStream<OllamaResponse>> {
    const requestData: OllamaRequest = {
      model,
      prompt,
      stream,
      options: {
        temperature: 0.7,
        num_predict: 512,
        top_p: 0.9,
        presence_penalty: 0.0,
        frequency_penalty: 0.0,
        stop: ["</s>", "\n\n"],
        ...options
      }
    };
    
    console.log(`[OllamaBridge] Querying ${model} with ${prompt.length} chars, streaming: ${stream}`);
    
    try {
      if (stream) {
        return this.streamOllama(requestData);
      } else {
        return await this.syncOllama(requestData);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      const classifiedError = this.classifyError(error);
      console.error(`[OllamaBridge] Query failed:`, classifiedError);
      
      // Try fallback if enabled
      if (config.enableFallback && config.geminiApiKey) {
        console.warn(`[OllamaBridge] Attempting Gemini fallback`);
        return await this.fallbackToGemini(prompt, model, classifiedError);
      }
      
      throw new Error(`Ollama query failed: ${classifiedError.message}`);
    }
  }
  
  private async syncOllama(requestData: OllamaRequest): Promise<OllamaResponse> {
    const response = await axios.post(
      `${config.ollamaHost}/api/generate`,
      requestData,
      {
        timeout: config.ollamaTimeout,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
      }
    );
    
    return this.parseResponse(response.data, requestData.model);
  }
  
  private streamOllama(requestData: OllamaRequest): ReadableStream<OllamaResponse> {
    return new ReadableStream({
      start: async (controller) => {
        try {
          const response = await fetch(`${config.ollamaHost}/api/generate`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify(requestData)
          });
          
          if (!response.body) {
            throw new Error('No response body from Ollama');
          }
          
          const reader = response.body.getReader();
          const decoder = new TextDecoder();
          let buffer = '';
          let fullResponse = '';
          
          while (true) {
            const { done, value } = await reader.read();
            
            if (done) {
              controller.enqueue({
                model: requestData.model,
                response: fullResponse,
                done: true
              });
              controller.close();
              break;
            }
            
            const chunk = decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
            buffer += chunk;
            
            const lines = buffer.split('\n');
            buffer = lines.pop() || '';
            
            for (const line of lines) {
              if (line.trim()) {
                try {
                  const parsed = JSON.parse(line);
                  if (parsed.response) {
                    fullResponse += parsed.response;
                    controller.enqueue({
                      model: requestData.model,
                      response: parsed.response,
                      done: parsed.done || false
                    });
                  }
                } catch (e) {
                  console.warn('Failed to parse streaming chunk:', line);
                }
              }
            }
          }
        } catch (error) {
          controller.error(error);
        }
      }
    });
  }
  
  // Enhanced model discovery with metadata
  async discoverModels(forceRefresh: boolean = false): Promise<ModelMetadata[]> {
    const now = Date.now();
    const cacheValid = (now - this.lastModelUpdate) < 300000; // 5 minutes
    
    if (!forceRefresh && this.models.length > 0 && cacheValid) {
      return this.models;
    }
    
    try {
      const response = await axios.get(`${config.ollamaHost}/api/tags`, {
        timeout: 5000
      });
      
      if (response.data?.models) {
        this.models = response.data.models.map((model: any) => ({
          name: model.name,
          digest: model.digest,
          details: {
            format: model.details?.format,
            family: model.details?.family || model.name.split(':')[0],
            parameter_size: model.details?.parameter_size,
            quantization: model.details?.quantization_level,
            modified_at: model.modified_at,
            size: model.size
          }
        }));
        
        this.lastModelUpdate = now;
        console.log(`[OllamaBridge] Discovered ${this.models.length} models`);
      }
      
      return this.models;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('[OllamaBridge] Failed to discover models:', error);
      return [];
    }
  }
  
  // Enhanced error classification
  private classifyError(error: any): {
    type: 'connection' | 'timeout' | 'model_not_found' | 'api_error' | 'rate_limit' | 'parsing';
    message: string;
    details?: any;
  } {
    const axiosError = error as AxiosError;
    
    if (axiosError.code === 'ECONNREFUSED') {
      return {
        type: 'connection',
        message: 'Cannot connect to Ollama. Ensure it is running at http://localhost:11434',
        details: { host: config.ollamaHost }
      };
    }
    
    if (axiosError.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') {
      return {
        type: 'timeout',
        message: `Ollama request timed out after ${config.ollamaTimeout}ms`,
        details: { timeout: config.ollamaTimeout }
      };
    }
    
    if (axiosError.response?.status === 404) {
      return {
        type: 'model_not_found',
        message: `Model not found. Run: ollama pull <model-name>`,
        details: { status: 404 }
      };
    }
    
    if (axiosError.response?.status === 429) {
      return {
        type: 'rate_limit',
        message: 'Ollama rate limit exceeded. Please wait before trying again.',
        details: { status: 429 }
      };
    }
    
    // Parse error from response body
    if (axiosError.response?.data) {
      try {
        const errorData = typeof axiosError.response.data === 'string'
          ? JSON.parse(axiosError.response.data)
          : axiosError.response.data;
        
        return {
          type: 'api_error',
          message: errorData.error || 'Ollama API error',
          details: errorData
        };
      } catch (parseError) {
        // Continue to default
      }
    }
    
    return {
      type: 'api_error',
      message: error.message || 'Unknown Ollama error',
      details: error
    };
  }
  
  private async fallbackToGemini(
    prompt: string,
    originalModel: string,
    ollamaError: any
  ): Promise<OllamaResponse> {
    console.warn(`[OllamaBridge] Falling back to Gemini. Original error: ${ollamaError.message}`);
    
    try {
      const enhancedPrompt = `
[Context: This query was intended for local Ollama model "${originalModel}" 
but failed due to: ${ollamaError.message}]

[Instructions: Provide a response as if you were the local AI model.]

Original query: ${prompt}`;
      
      // Gemini API call would go here
      // For now, return simulated response
      return {
        model: 'gemini-pro (fallback)',
        response: `[Gemini Fallback] Processed query: ${prompt.substring(0, 100)}...`,
        done: true
      };
      
    } catch (fallbackError) {
      console.error('[OllamaBridge] Gemini fallback also failed:', fallbackError);
      throw new Error(`All fallbacks failed: ${ollamaError.message}`);
    }
  }
  
  private parseResponse(data: any, model: string): OllamaResponse {
    // Handle various response formats
    let responseText = '';
    let done = true;
    
    if (typeof data === 'string') {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
        responseText = parsed.response || parsed.text || '';
        done = parsed.done !== false;
      } catch {
        responseText = data;
      }
    } else if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
      responseText = data.response || data.text || data.content || '';
      done = data.done !== false;
    }
    
    return {
      model,
      response: responseText,
      done,
      context: data.context,
      total_duration: data.total_duration,
      load_duration: data.load_duration,
      prompt_eval_count: data.prompt_eval_count,
      eval_count: data.eval_count
    };
  }
  
  // Test functions
  async testConnection(model?: string): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    details?: any;
  }> {
    try {
      // Test connectivity
      await axios.get(`${config.ollamaHost}/api/tags`, { timeout: 3000 });
      
      // Test model response
      const testModel = model || (this.models.length > 0 ? this.models[0].name : 'llama3');
      const response = await this.queryOllama('Respond with "READY"', testModel, { temperature: 0.1 });
      
      const isReady = (response as OllamaResponse).response?.includes('READY');
      
      return {
        success: isReady,
        message: isReady ? `Model ${testModel} is ready` : 'Model responded but not as expected',
        details: {
          model: testModel,
          response: (response as OllamaResponse).response
        }
      };
    } catch (error) {
      const classified = this.classifyError(error);
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Connection test failed: ${classified.message}`,
        details: classified
      };
    }
  }
  
  // Simulate various error conditions for testing
  async simulateError(errorType: string): Promise<void> {
    const errors = {
      connection: () => { throw new Error('ECONNREFUSED'); },
      timeout: () => { throw new Error('ETIMEDOUT'); },
      model_not_found: () => { throw new Error('Model not found'); },
      rate_limit: () => { throw new Error('Rate limit exceeded'); }
    };
    
    if (errors[errorType as keyof typeof errors]) {
      errors[errorType as keyof typeof errors]();
    }
  }
}

// Enhanced Agent Response Parser
export class AgentResponseParser {
  static parseAgentResponse(
    rawResponse: string,
    expectedAgent: string,
    streaming: boolean = false,
    chunks: string[] = []
  ): AgentResponse {
    const strategies = [
      this.parseStructuredJson,
      this.parseMarkdownWithHeaders,
      this.parseKeyValueFormat,
      this.parseNaturalLanguage
    ];
    
    for (const strategy of strategies) {
      try {
        const parsed = strategy(rawResponse, expectedAgent);
        if (parsed && parsed.decision && parsed.reasoning) {
          return {
            ...parsed,
            rawResponse,
            streaming,
            chunks,
            confidence: parsed.confidence || this.calculateConfidence(parsed, chunks)
          };
        }
      } catch {
        continue;
      }
    }
    
    return this.createFallbackResponse(rawResponse, expectedAgent, streaming, chunks);
  }
  
  // Enhanced CODE agent prompt generator
  static generateCODEAgentPrompt(
    prompt: string,
    signInsights?: string,
    loopOptimizations?: string,
    corePrinciples?: string
  ): string {
    return `
[ROLE: CODE SYNTHESIZER - Interpreter & Executor]
[PRECEDENCE: CORE > CODE > LOOP > SIGN]

CONTEXT:
${corePrinciples ? `CORE PRINCIPLES:\n${corePrinciples}\n` : ''}
${signInsights ? `SIGN INTERPRETATIONS:\n${signInsights}\n` : 'Awaiting SIGN interpretation...'}
${loopOptimizations ? `LOOP OPTIMIZATIONS:\n${loopOptimizations}\n` : 'Awaiting LOOP optimization patterns...'}

INSTRUCTIONS:
1. Synthesize findings from all agents
2. Prioritize CORE principles when conflicts arise
3. Transform insights into executable code
4. Handle partial dependencies gracefully
5. Support fallback to Gemini if Ollama fails
6. Parse varied response formats

TASK: ${prompt}

RESPONSE FORMAT:
{
  "agent": "CODE",
  "decision": "Specific implementation approach",
  "reasoning": "Synthesis of all agent inputs",
  "confidence": 0.0-1.0,
  "dependencies": ["SIGN", "LOOP", "CORE"],
  "implementation": "Code snippet or pseudocode"
}

BEGIN SYNTHESIS:
`;
  }
  
  private static parseStructuredJson(
    rawResponse: string,
    expectedAgent: string
  ): Partial<AgentResponse> | null {
    const jsonMatch = rawResponse.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
    if (!jsonMatch) return null;
    
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
      return {
        agent: parsed.agent || parsed.role || expectedAgent,
        decision: parsed.decision || parsed.result || parsed.output || parsed.implementation || '',
        reasoning: parsed.reasoning || parsed.analysis || parsed.explanation || parsed.synthesis || '',
        confidence: parsed.confidence || parsed.certainty || 0.5
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
  
  private static parseMarkdownWithHeaders(
    rawResponse: string,
    expectedAgent: string
  ): Partial<AgentResponse> | null {
    const decisionMatch = rawResponse.match(/##? (?:Decision|Implementation)[:\s]*\n*(.*?)(?=\n##? |$)/is);
    const reasoningMatch = rawResponse.match(/##? (?:Reasoning|Synthesis)[:\s]*\n*(.*?)(?=\n##? |$)/is);
    
    if (!decisionMatch && !reasoningMatch) return null;
    
    return {
      agent: expectedAgent,
      decision: decisionMatch?.[1]?.trim() || '',
      reasoning: reasoningMatch?.[1]?.trim() || '',
      confidence: this.extractConfidenceFromText(rawResponse)
    };
  }
  
  private static parseKeyValueFormat(
    rawResponse: string,
    expectedAgent: string
  ): Partial<AgentResponse> | null {
    const lines = rawResponse.split('\n');
    let decision = '';
    let reasoning = '';
    
    for (const line of lines) {
      const lowerLine = line.toLowerCase();
      if (lowerLine.includes('decision:') || lowerLine.includes('implementation:')) {
        decision = line.split(':').slice(1).join(':').trim();
      } else if (lowerLine.includes('reasoning:') || lowerLine.includes('synthesis:')) {
        reasoning = line.split(':').slice(1).join(':').trim();
      }
    }
    
    if (!decision && !reasoning) return null;
    
    return {
      agent: expectedAgent,
      decision,
      reasoning,
      confidence: this.extractConfidenceFromText(rawResponse)
    };
  }
  
  private static parseNaturalLanguage(
    rawResponse: string,
    expectedAgent: string
  ): Partial<AgentResponse> | null {
    const sentences = rawResponse.split(/[.!?]+/).filter(s => s.trim());
    if (sentences.length === 0) return null;
    
    return {
      agent: expectedAgent,
      decision: sentences[0].trim(),
      reasoning: sentences.slice(1).join('. ').trim() || sentences[0].trim(),
      confidence: 0.3
    };
  }
  
  private static extractConfidenceFromText(text: string): number {
    const patterns = [
      /confidence[:\s]*(\d+\.?\d*)/i,
      /certainty[:\s]*(\d+\.?\d*)/i,
      /(\d+)% (confidence|certainty)/i,
      /I am (\d+)% sure/i
    ];
    
    for (const pattern of patterns) {
      const match = text.match(pattern);
      if (match) {
        const value = parseFloat(match[1]);
        if (!isNaN(value)) {
          return value > 1 ? value / 100 : value;
        }
      }
    }
    
    return 0.5;
  }
  
  private static calculateConfidence(parsed: Partial<AgentResponse>, chunks: string[]): number {
    let confidence = 0.5;
    
    // Longer reasoning suggests more confidence
    if (parsed.reasoning && parsed.reasoning.length > 100) confidence += 0.2;
    
    // Clear decision suggests more confidence
    if (parsed.decision && parsed.decision.length > 20) confidence += 0.2;
    
    // Multiple streaming chunks suggests thorough processing
    if (chunks.length > 3) confidence += 0.1;
    
    // Contains code or specific references
    if (parsed.decision?.match(/```|\b(def|function|class)\b/)) confidence += 0.1;
    
    return Math.min(0.95, confidence);
  }
  
  private static createFallbackResponse(
    rawResponse: string,
    expectedAgent: string,
    streaming: boolean,
    chunks: string[]
  ): AgentResponse {
    return {
      agent: expectedAgent,
      decision: 'Unable to parse specific decision',
      reasoning: streaming 
        ? `Streaming response assembled from ${chunks.length} chunks`
        : `Raw response: ${rawResponse.substring(0, 200)}...`,
      confidence: 0.1,
      rawResponse,
      streaming,
      chunks
    };
  }
}

// Enhanced Nexus Engine
export class NexusEngine {
  private ollamaBridge: OllamaBridge;
  private activeModel: string = 'llama3';
  private streamingEnabled: boolean = false;
  
  constructor() {
    this.ollamaBridge = OllamaBridge.getInstance();
  }
  
  // Enhanced processPrompt with streaming support
  async processPrompt(
    prompt: string,
    options: {
      model?: string;
      agent?: string;
      stream?: boolean;
      context?: any[];
    } = {}
  ): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    result: any;
    agentDecisions: AgentResponse[];
    fallbackUsed: boolean;
    errors: string[];
    streaming?: boolean;
  }> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const errors: string[] = [];
    let fallbackUsed = false;
    
    // Update active model if specified
    if (options.model) {
      this.activeModel = options.model;
    }
    
    this.streamingEnabled = options.stream || false;
    
    console.log(`[NexusEngine] Processing prompt with ${this.activeModel}, streaming: ${this.streamingEnabled}`);
    
    try {
      // Determine which agent to use
      const targetAgent = options.agent || 'CODE';
      
      // Enhanced CODE agent prompt with dependencies
      let enhancedPrompt = prompt;
      if (targetAgent === 'CODE') {
        enhancedPrompt = AgentResponseParser.generateCODEAgentPrompt(
          prompt,
          options.context?.[0]?.signInsights,
          options.context?.[0]?.loopOptimizations,
          options.context?.[0]?.corePrinciples
        );
      }
      
      // Query Ollama
      let response: OllamaResponse | ReadableStream<OllamaResponse>;
      let streamingResult = '';
      const chunks: string[] = [];
      
      try {
        response = await this.ollamaBridge.queryOllama(
          enhancedPrompt,
          this.activeModel,
          this.getAgentOptions(targetAgent),
          this.streamingEnabled
        );
        
        if (this.streamingEnabled && response instanceof ReadableStream) {
          // Handle streaming response
          const reader = response.getReader();
          
          while (true) {
            const { done, value } = await reader.read();
            
            if (done) {
              break;
            }
            
            chunks.push(value.response);
            streamingResult += value.response;
            
            // Emit chunk event for UI
            if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ollama-chunk', {
                detail: {
                  chunk: value.response,
                  agent: targetAgent,
                  done: value.done
                }
              }));
            }
          }
          
          response = {
            model: this.activeModel,
            response: streamingResult,
            done: true
          };
        }
      } catch (ollamaError) {
        console.error('[NexusEngine] Ollama query failed, attempting fallback:', ollamaError);
        fallbackUsed = true;
        
        // Enhanced fallback logging
        console.warn(`[NexusEngine] Fallback triggered. Details:`, {
          originalModel: this.activeModel,
          error: ollamaError.message,
          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
          promptLength: prompt.length
        });
        
        // Try Gemini fallback
        try {
          response = await this.ollamaBridge['fallbackToGemini'](
            prompt,
            this.activeModel,
            ollamaError
          );
        } catch (fallbackError) {
          errors.push(`All fallbacks failed: ${fallbackError.message}`);
          throw fallbackError;
        }
      }
      
      const ollamaResponse = response as OllamaResponse;
      
      // Parse agent response
      const agentResponse = AgentResponseParser.parseAgentResponse(
        ollamaResponse.response,
        targetAgent,
        this.streamingEnabled,
        chunks
      );
      
      const processingTime = Date.now() - startTime;
      
      console.log(`[NexusEngine] Processing completed in ${processingTime}ms`);
      if (fallbackUsed) {
        console.warn(`[NexusEngine] Fallback mechanism was used`);
      }
      
      return {
        success: true,
        result: ollamaResponse.response,
        agentDecisions: [agentResponse],
        fallbackUsed,
        errors,
        streaming: this.streamingEnabled
      };
      
    } catch (error) {
      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      errors.push(`Processing failed: ${errorMessage}`);
      
      console.error('[NexusEngine] Processing failed:', error);
      
      return {
        success: false,
        result: 'All processing attempts failed',
        agentDecisions: [],
        fallbackUsed,
        errors
      };
    }
  }
  
  private getAgentOptions(agent: string): any {
    const baseOptions = {
      temperature: 0.7,
      num_predict: 512,
      top_p: 0.9,
      presence_penalty: 0.0,
      frequency_penalty: 0.0,
      stop: ["</s>", "\n\n"]
    };
    
    // Agent-specific tuning
    const agentOptions: Record<string, any> = {
      CODE: { temperature: 0.3, num_predict: 1024 },
      CORE: { temperature: 0.2, num_predict: 768 },
      LOOP: { temperature: 0.5, num_predict: 512 },
      SIGN: { temperature: 0.8, num_predict: 384 },
      WORK: { temperature: 0.4, num_predict: 896 }
    };
    
    return { ...baseOptions, ...(agentOptions[agent] || {}) };
  }
  
  // Enhanced model management
  async getModels(): Promise<ModelMetadata[]> {
    return this.ollamaBridge.discoverModels();
  }
  
  setActiveModel(model: string): void {
    this.activeModel = model;
    console.log(`[NexusEngine] Active model set to: ${model}`);
  }
  
  getActiveModel(): string {
    return this.activeModel;
  }
  
  // Test functions
  async testOllamaIntegration(): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    details?: any;
  }> {
    return this.ollamaBridge.testConnection(this.activeModel);
  }
  
  async simulateAndTestFallback(): Promise<void> {
    console.log('[NexusEngine] Testing fallback mechanisms...');
    
    const testCases = [
      'connection',
      'timeout',
      'model_not_found',
      'rate_limit'
    ];
    
    for (const errorType of testCases) {
      console.log(`Testing ${errorType} fallback...`);
      try {
        await this.ollamaBridge['simulateError'](errorType);
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(`Simulated ${errorType}:`, error.message);
        
        // Test that processPrompt handles it gracefully
        const result = await this.processPrompt('Test prompt', { 
          model: 'nonexistent-model',
          agent: 'CODE'
        });
        
        console.log(`Fallback result:`, {
          success: result.success,
          fallbackUsed: result.fallbackUsed,
          errors: result.errors
        });
      }
    }
    
    console.log('[NexusEngine] Fallback tests completed');
  }
}

// Export singleton
export const nexusEngine = new NexusEngine();

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { nexusEngine } from '../services/nexusEngine';

interface ModelMetadata {
  name: string;
  digest: string;
  details: {
    format?: string;
    family?: string;
    parameter_size?: string;
    quantization?: string;
    modified_at?: string;
    size?: number;
  };
}

interface StreamingChunk {
  chunk: string;
  agent: string;
  done: boolean;
}

const App: React.FC = () => {
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('');
  const [response, setResponse] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [models, setModels] = useState<ModelMetadata[]>([]);
  const [selectedModel, setSelectedModel] = useState('');
  const [modelDetails, setModelDetails] = useState<ModelMetadata | null>(null);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [streaming, setStreaming] = useState(false);
  const [streamChunks, setStreamChunks] = useState<StreamingChunk[]>([]);
  const [activeStream, setActiveStream] = useState<string>('');
  const streamContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Load models on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    loadModels();
    
    // Listen for streaming chunks
    const handleChunk = (event: CustomEvent) => {
      const chunk = event.detail as StreamingChunk;
      setStreamChunks(prev => [...prev, chunk]);
      
      if (chunk.done) {
        setStreaming(false);
        setActiveStream('');
      }
      
      // Auto-scroll
      if (streamContainerRef.current) {
        streamContainerRef.current.scrollTop = streamContainerRef.current.scrollHeight;
      }
    };
    
    window.addEventListener('ollama-chunk', handleChunk as EventListener);
    
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('ollama-chunk', handleChunk as EventListener);
    };
  }, []);

  const loadModels = async () => {
    try {
      const availableModels = await nexusEngine.getModels();
      setModels(availableModels);
      
      if (availableModels.length > 0) {
        const defaultModel = availableModels.find(m => m.name.includes('llama'))?.name || availableModels[0].name;
        setSelectedModel(defaultModel);
        nexusEngine.setActiveModel(defaultModel);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load models:', error);
      setErrors(prev => [...prev, 'Failed to load models']);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    if (!prompt.trim()) return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    setResponse('');
    setErrors([]);
    setStreamChunks([]);
    
    // Update active model
    if (selectedModel) {
      nexusEngine.setActiveModel(selectedModel);
    }
    
    console.log(`[App] Processing prompt with model: ${selectedModel}, streaming: ${streaming}`);
    
    try {
      const result = await nexusEngine.processPrompt(prompt, {
        model: selectedModel,
        agent: 'CODE',
        stream: streaming,
        context: [{
          signInsights: 'Symbolic interpretation of patterns',
          loopOptimizations: 'Iterative refinement patterns',
          corePrinciples: 'Fundamental logical axioms'
        }]
      });
      
      setResponse(result.result);
      
      if (result.fallbackUsed) {
        console.warn(`[App] Fallback was triggered. Details:`, {
          originalModel: selectedModel,
          errors: result.errors,
          timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
        });
        
        setErrors(prev => [...prev, 'Fallback to Gemini was used']);
      }
      
      if (result.errors.length > 0) {
        setErrors(prev => [...prev, ...result.errors]);
      }
      
    } catch (error) {
      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      setErrors(prev => [...prev, `Processing failed: ${errorMessage}`]);
      console.error('[App] Processing failed:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleModelSelect = (modelName: string) => {
    setSelectedModel(modelName);
    const model = models.find(m => m.name === modelName);
    setModelDetails(model || null);
  };

  // Assemble streaming chunks into readable text
  const streamingText = streamChunks
    .filter(chunk => chunk.agent === activeStream)
    .map(chunk => chunk.chunk)
    .join('');

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-900 text-gray-100 p-6">
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto">
        <header className="mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-400">NEXUS AI Orchestrator</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2">Multi-Agent System with Streaming Support</p>
        </header>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {/* Left Panel - Controls */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-1 space-y-6">
            {/* Model Selector */}
            <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-3">🧠 Ollama Models</h3>
              
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <select
                  value={selectedModel}
                  onChange={(e) => handleModelSelect(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  disabled={loading}
                >
                  <option value="">Select a model...</option>
                  {models.map(model => (
                    <option key={model.name} value={model.name}>
                      {model.name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
                
                {modelDetails && (
                  <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded p-3 text-sm space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex justify-between">
                      <span className="text-gray-400">Family:</span>
                      <span>{modelDetails.details.family || 'Unknown'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between">
                      <span className="text-gray-400">Digest:</span>
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs">
                        {modelDetails.digest.substring(0, 16)}...
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {modelDetails.details.parameter_size && (
                      <div className="flex justify-between">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">Parameters:</span>
                        <span>{modelDetails.details.parameter_size}</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {modelDetails.details.quantization && (
                      <div className="flex justify-between">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">Quantization:</span>
                        <span>{modelDetails.details.quantization}</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {modelDetails.details.modified_at && (
                      <div className="flex justify-between">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">Modified:</span>
                        <span>
                          {new Date(modelDetails.details.modified_at).toLocaleDateString()}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              
              <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-700">
                <label className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={streaming}
                    onChange={(e) => setStreaming(e.target.checked)}
                    className="rounded"
                  />
                  <span>Enable Streaming</span>
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            {/* Status */}
            <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-3">📊 Status</h3>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-gray-400">Active Model:</span>
                  <span className="font-medium">{selectedModel || 'None'}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-gray-400">Streaming:</span>
                  <span className={streaming ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-yellow-400'}>
                    {streaming ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-gray-400">Available Models:</span>
                  <span>{models.length}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          {/* Main Panel - Input & Output */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
            {/* Input Form */}
            <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">
                    Enter Prompt for CODE Agent
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    value={prompt}
                    onChange={(e) => setPrompt(e.target.value)}
                    rows={4}
                    className="w-full bg-gray-700 border border-gray-600 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                    placeholder="Describe what you want the CODE agent to implement..."
                    disabled={loading}
                  />
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                    The CODE agent will synthesize insights from SIGN, LOOP, and CORE agents
                  </p>
                </div>
                
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading || !prompt.trim()}
                    className="px-6 py-2 bg-green-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    {loading ? 'Processing...' : 'Send to CODE Agent'}
                  </button>
                  
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => nexusEngine.simulateAndTestFallback()}
                    className="px-4 py-2 bg-yellow-600 text-white rounded hover:bg-yellow-700 text-sm"
                  >
                    Test Fallback
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            </div>
            
            {/* Streaming Output */}
            {streaming && streamChunks.length > 0 && (
              <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-3">⚡ Live Stream</h3>
                <div
                  ref={streamContainerRef}
                  className="bg-gray-900 rounded p-4 h-64 overflow-y-auto font-mono text-sm"
                >
                  {streamingText.split('').map((char, index) => (
                    <span key={index} className="text-green-300">
                      {char}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                  {streaming && !streamChunks[streamChunks.length - 1]?.done && (
                    <span className="animate-pulse">▊</span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            
            {/* Response */}
            {response && (
              <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-3">🤖 Response</h3>
                <div className="bg-gray-900 rounded p-4 whitespace-pre-wrap font-mono text-sm">
                  {response}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            
            {/* Errors */}
            {errors.length > 0 && (
              <div className="bg-red-900/30 border border-red-700 rounded-lg p-4">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-red-300 mb-2">⚠️ Errors</h3>
                <ul className="space-y-1">
                  {errors.map((error, index) => (
                    <li key={index} className="text-sm text-red-200">
                      • {error}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default App;

// Test script for Ollama fallback mechanisms
const { nexusEngine } = require('./services/nexusEngine');

async function runTests() {
  console.log('🚀 Starting Ollama Fallback Tests');
  console.log('================================');
  
  // Test 1: Normal query
  console.log('\n📝 Test 1: Normal Ollama Query');
  try {
    const result = await nexusEngine.processPrompt('Hello, respond with OK', {
      model: 'llama3',
      stream: false
    });
    
    console.log(`✓ Success: ${result.success}`);
    console.log(`  Fallback used: ${result.fallbackUsed}`);
    console.log(`  Response length: ${result.result.length}`);
  } catch (error) {
    console.log(`✗ Failed: ${error.message}`);
  }
  
  // Test 2: Simulated connection error
  console.log('\n📝 Test 2: Simulated Connection Error');
  try {
    // Temporarily change Ollama host to simulate error
    process.env.OLLAMA_HOST = 'http://localhost:9999';
    
    const result = await nexusEngine.processPrompt('Test connection error', {
      model: 'llama3',
      stream: false
    });
    
    console.log(`✓ Handled gracefully`);
    console.log(`  Fallback used: ${result.fallbackUsed}`);
    console.log(`  Errors: ${result.errors.length}`);
  } catch (error) {
    console.log(`✗ Unhandled: ${error.message}`);
  } finally {
    // Restore original host
    process.env.OLLAMA_HOST = 'http://localhost:11434';
  }
  
  // Test 3: Streaming with simulated timeout
  console.log('\n📝 Test 3: Streaming with Timeout Simulation');
  try {
    // Mock a timeout scenario
    const originalTimeout = nexusEngine['ollamaBridge']['config'].ollamaTimeout;
    nexusEngine['ollamaBridge']['config'].ollamaTimeout = 1; // 1ms timeout
    
    const result = await nexusEngine.processPrompt('Test streaming timeout', {
      model: 'llama3',
      stream: true
    });
    
    console.log(`✓ Streaming test completed`);
    console.log(`  Success: ${result.success}`);
    console.log(`  Streaming: ${result.streaming}`);
  } catch (error) {
    console.log(`✗ Streaming failed: ${error.message}`);
  }
  
  // Test 4: CODE agent with dependencies
  console.log('\n📝 Test 4: CODE Agent Synthesis');
  try {
    const result = await nexusEngine.processPrompt('Create a Python function to calculate fibonacci', {
      agent: 'CODE',
      stream: false,
      context: [{
        signInsights: 'Fibonacci represents growth patterns and natural spirals',
        loopOptimizations: 'Use memoization for efficiency',
        corePrinciples: 'Ensure mathematical correctness and edge case handling'
      }]
    });
    
    console.log(`✓ CODE agent processed`);
    console.log(`  Decision confidence: ${result.agentDecisions[0]?.confidence || 'N/A'}`);
    console.log(`  Response contains code: ${result.result.includes('def ') || result.result.includes('function')}`);
  } catch (error) {
    console.log(`✗ CODE agent failed: ${error.message}`);
  }
  
  // Test 5: Model discovery and metadata
  console.log('\n📝 Test 5: Model Discovery');
  try {
    const models = await nexusEngine.getModels();
    console.log(`✓ Found ${models.length} models`);
    
    if (models.length > 0) {
      const sampleModel = models[0];
      console.log(`  Sample model: ${sampleModel.name}`);
      console.log(`  Family: ${sampleModel.details.family}`);
      console.log(`  Digest: ${sampleModel.digest.substring(0, 16)}...`);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.log(`✗ Model discovery failed: ${error.message}`);
  }
  
  console.log('\n✅ All tests completed');
  console.log('================================');
}

// Run tests
runTests().catch(console.error);

2. Add this block inside your userscript

Place it after class WSLBridge and before initializeNexus().

🔷 NEXUS MEMORY GRAPH ENGINE
class NexusMemoryGraph {
    constructor(bridge) {
        this.bridge = bridge;
        this.nodes = new Map();      // hash → node
        this.edges = new Set();      // parent → child
        this.scene = null;
        this.camera = null;
        this.renderer = null;
        this.meshes = new Map();
        this.selectedNode = null;
    }

    async loadMemories() {
        const memories = await this.bridge.getMemories(1000);
        for (const m of memories) {
            this.ingestMemory(m);
        }
    }

    ingestMemory(memory) {
        const g = memory.genesisHash?.genesis || memory.id;
        const node = {
            id: g,
            prompt: memory.prompt,
            entropy: memory.genesisHash?.entropy || 0,
            time: memory.timestamp,
            responses: memory.responses || [],
            best: memory.bestResponse,
            parent: memory.context?.genesisHash?.genesis || null
        };
        this.nodes.set(g, node);
        if (node.parent) this.edges.add([node.parent, g]);
    }

    /* ---------- Three.js ---------- */

    init3D(canvas) {
        this.scene = new THREE.Scene();
        this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, canvas.clientWidth / canvas.clientHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        this.camera.position.z = 40;

        this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, alpha: true });
        this.renderer.setSize(canvas.clientWidth, canvas.clientHeight);

        const light = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x00ff88, 1);
        light.position.set(1, 1, 1);
        this.scene.add(light);

        this.animate();
    }

    animate() {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => this.animate());
        this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera);
    }

    buildGraph() {
        this.meshes.forEach(m => this.scene.remove(m));
        this.meshes.clear();

        const layout = [...this.nodes.values()];
        layout.forEach((n, i) => {
            const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1 + n.entropy * 3, 16, 16);
            const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
                color: new THREE.Color().setHSL(0.35 - n.entropy * 0.35, 1, 0.5)
            });
            const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
            mesh.position.set(
                Math.sin(i) * 15,
                Math.cos(i * 1.7) * 10,
                i * -0.6
            );
            mesh.userData = n;
            this.scene.add(mesh);
            this.meshes.set(n.id, mesh);
        });

        // edges
        for (const [a, b] of this.edges) {
            const A = this.meshes.get(a);
            const B = this.meshes.get(b);
            if (!A || !B) continue;
            const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([A.position, B.position]);
            this.scene.add(new THREE.Line(g, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x006600 })));
        }
    }

    pick(ray) {
        const hits = ray.intersectObjects([...this.meshes.values()]);
        if (!hits.length) return;
        this.inspect(hits[0].object.userData);
    }

    inspect(node) {
        this.selectedNode = node;
        alert(
            `GENESIS: ${node.id}\n` +
            `Entropy: ${node.entropy}\n` +
            `Prompt: ${node.prompt}\n` +
            `Responses: ${node.responses.length}\n` +
            `Time: ${new Date(node.time).toLocaleString()}`
        );
    }

    async replay() {
        const sorted = [...this.nodes.values()].sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);
        for (const n of sorted) {
            console.log("⏳ REPLAY", n.prompt);
            await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));
        }
    }

    async export() {
        const blob = new Blob(
            [JSON.stringify([...this.nodes.values()], null, 2)],
            { type: "application/json" }
        );
        GM_download({ url: URL.createObjectURL(blob), name: "nexus_memory_graph.json" });
    }
}

async captureDOMFingerprint() {
    const html = document.documentElement.outerHTML;
    const text = html.replace(/\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 200000);
    const hash = await this.entropyManager.sha256(text);
    return {
        hash,
        length: text.length,
        sample: text.slice(0, 2000)
    };
}

inspect(node) {
    let diff = "No DOM change";
    if (node.domBefore && node.domAfter && node.domBefore.hash !== node.domAfter.hash) {
        diff = `Δ DOM changed
Before: ${node.domBefore.hash}
After : ${node.domAfter.hash}
Size: ${node.domAfter.length - node.domBefore.length}`;
    }

    alert(
        `GENESIS: ${node.id}
Entropy: ${node.entropy}
Prompt: ${node.prompt}
Responses: ${node.responses.length}
Time: ${new Date(node.time).toLocaleString()}

${diff}`
    );
}

🧠 DOM-DIFF HEATMAP ENGINE

We will generate structural DOM deltas and paint them directly into the 3D memory graph.

Each Genesis-Hash node now becomes a map of physical web-page change.

1️⃣ Capture DOM structure as a tree

Add to WSLBridge:

captureDOMTree() {
    function walk(node, path = "0") {
        let out = [];
        if (!node || !node.tagName) return out;

        const id = node.id || "";
        const cls = node.className || "";
        const sig = node.tagName + "#" + id + "." + cls;

        out.push({ path, sig });

        Array.from(node.children).forEach((child, i) => {
            out.push(...walk(child, path + "." + i));
        });

        return out;
    }

    return walk(document.body);
}

2️⃣ Compute per-node deltas

Add:

diffDOMTrees(before, after) {
    const mapA = new Map(before.map(n => [n.path, n.sig]));
    const mapB = new Map(after.map(n => [n.path, n.sig]));

    const changes = [];

    for (const [path, sig] of mapB.entries()) {
        if (!mapA.has(path)) {
            changes.push({ path, type: "added", sig });
        } else if (mapA.get(path) !== sig) {
            changes.push({ path, type: "modified", from: mapA.get(path), to: sig });
        }
    }

    for (const [path, sig] of mapA.entries()) {
        if (!mapB.has(path)) {
            changes.push({ path, type: "removed", sig });
        }
    }

    return changes;
}

3️⃣ Store spatial DOM deltas in memory

In processPrompt():

Before agent execution:

const domTreeBefore = this.captureDOMTree();


After:

const domTreeAfter = this.captureDOMTree();
const domDiff = this.diffDOMTrees(domTreeBefore, domTreeAfter);


Store:

await this.storeMemory({
    prompt,
    responses: validResults,
    bestResponse,
    context,
    genesisHash,
    domTreeBefore,
    domTreeAfter,
    domDiff,
    timestamp: Date.now()
});

🔥 LIVE PAGE DOM HEATMAP OVERLAY

We will paint AI-caused DOM changes directly onto the active webpage.

Red = modified
Green = added
Blue = removed

With intensity proportional to entropy & structural impact.

1️⃣ Create overlay container

Add once during initialization:

function createHeatmapLayer() {
    const layer = document.createElement("div");
    layer.id = "nexus-heatmap";
    Object.assign(layer.style, {
        position: "fixed",
        left: "0",
        top: "0",
        width: "100vw",
        height: "100vh",
        pointerEvents: "none",
        zIndex: 999999,
        mixBlendMode: "screen"
    });
    document.body.appendChild(layer);
}


Call inside initializeNexus().

2️⃣ Find DOM nodes from diff paths

Add:

getNodeByPath(path) {
    try {
        let node = document.body;
        path.split(".").slice(1).forEach(i => {
            node = node.children[parseInt(i)];
        });
        return node;
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

3️⃣ Draw heat over DOM nodes

Add:

renderDOMHeatmap(domDiff) {
    const layer = document.getElementById("nexus-heatmap");
    layer.innerHTML = "";

    domDiff.forEach(change => {
        const el = this.getNodeByPath(change.path);
        if (!el) return;

        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        const box = document.createElement("div");

        let color = "rgba(255,255,0,0.3)";
        if (change.type === "added") color = "rgba(0,255,0,0.4)";
        if (change.type === "modified") color = "rgba(255,0,0,0.4)";
        if (change.type === "removed") color = "rgba(0,0,255,0.4)";

        Object.assign(box.style, {
            position: "absolute",
            left: rect.left + "px",
            top: rect.top + "px",
            width: rect.width + "px",
            height: rect.height + "px",
            background: color,
            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)",
            borderRadius: "4px",
            animation: "pulse 2s infinite"
        });

        layer.appendChild(box);
    });
}

4️⃣ Trigger heatmap after each prompt

At end of processPrompt():

this.renderDOMHeatmap(domDiff);

5️⃣ Add animation

Add via GM_addStyle:

@keyframes pulse {
  0% { opacity: 0.2; }
  50% { opacity: 0.8; }
  100% { opacity: 0.2; }
}

🧬 1) NEXUS CORE — Node.js Backend

Create this file on your WSL1 Debian:

~/nexus/server.js

import express from "express";
import bodyParser from "body-parser";
import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import crypto from "crypto";
import fetch from "node-fetch";

const app = express();
app.use(bodyParser.json({limit:"50mb"}));

const db = new Database("nexus.db");

db.exec(`
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  parent TEXT,
  time INTEGER,
  agent TEXT,
  prompt TEXT,
  response TEXT,
  domdiff TEXT,
  entropy REAL
);
`);

function hash(x){
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(x).digest("hex");
}

async function ollama(prompt){
  const r = await fetch("http://localhost:11434/api/generate",{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{ "Content-Type":"application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ model:"gemma3:1b", prompt, stream:false })
  });
  return (await r.json()).response;
}

let lastNode = null;

app.post("/event", async (req,res)=>{
  const {dom, diff, prompt} = req.body;

  const response = await ollama(
    `You are NEXUS. Analyze DOM and diff.\nDOM:\n${dom}\nDIFF:\n${JSON.stringify(diff)}\nUser:${prompt}`
  );

  const entropy = diff.length + response.length;
  const id = hash(Date.now()+response);

  db.prepare(`INSERT INTO memory VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`)
    .run(id,lastNode,Date.now(),"CORE",prompt,response,JSON.stringify(diff),entropy);

  lastNode = id;

  res.json({response,id});
});

app.get("/memory", (req,res)=>{
  res.json(db.prepare("SELECT * FROM memory").all());
});

app.listen(3000, ()=>console.log("NEXUS online on :3000"));


Run:

node server.js

🧠 2) Tampermonkey Injection Layer

Install this as a Tampermonkey script.

// ==UserScript==
// @name         NEXUS 2244-1
// @namespace    http://nexus.local
// @version      1.0
// @match        *://*/*
// @grant        none
// ==/UserScript==

(function(){
  let lastDOM = document.body.innerHTML;

  function diffDOM(a,b){
    return a===b ? [] : [{change:"modified", entropy: Math.abs(a.length-b.length)}];
  }

  function injectUI(){
    const frame = document.createElement("iframe");
    frame.style.position="fixed";
    frame.style.bottom="0";
    frame.style.left="0";
    frame.style.width="100%";
    frame.style.height="300px";
    frame.style.zIndex="999999";
    frame.srcdoc = `
      <html>
      <body style="background:#000;color:#0f0">
        <textarea id="p" style="width:100%;height:80px"></textarea>
        <button onclick="send()">Send to NEXUS</button>
        <pre id="out"></pre>
        <script>
          function send(){
            parent.postMessage(document.getElementById("p").value,"*");
          }
        </script>
      </body>
      </html>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(frame);
  }

  injectUI();

  window.addEventListener("message", async e=>{
    const dom = document.body.innerHTML;
    const diff = diffDOM(lastDOM,dom);
    lastDOM = dom;

    const r = await fetch("http://localhost:3000/event",{
      method:"POST",
      headers:{ "Content-Type":"application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        dom,
        diff,
        prompt: e.data
      })
    });

    const j = await r.json();
    alert("NEXUS:\n"+j.response);
  });
})();

🧬 1️⃣ Multi-Agent Brain (Server Upgrade)

Replace your Ollama call in server.js with a swarm:

const models = ["gemma3:1b","mistral","neural-chat"];

async function swarm(prompt){
  const votes = [];
  for(const m of models){
    const r = await fetch("http://localhost:11434/api/generate",{
      method:"POST",
      headers:{ "Content-Type":"application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ model:m, prompt, stream:false })
    });
    const j = await r.json();
    votes.push({model:m, text:j.response});
  }

  // consensus
  return votes.sort((a,b)=>b.text.length-a.text.length)[0];
}


Then in /event:

const {text:response} = await swarm(fullPrompt);


This gives distributed cognition.

<script>
fetch("http://localhost:3000/memory").then(r=>r.json()).then(nodes=>{
 const scene=new THREE.Scene();

 cam.position.z=50;
 const renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer();
 renderer.setSize(innerWidth,innerHeight);
 document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

 nodes.forEach((n,i)=>{
   const g=new THREE.SphereGeometry(1);
   const m=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x00ffcc});
   const s=new THREE.Mesh(g,m);
   s.position.set(i*2,Math.random()*5,0);
   scene.add(s);
 });

 function animate(){ requestAnimationFrame(animate); renderer.render(scene,cam); }
 animate();
});
</script>

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# == Gemini-CLI Installer: NEXUS AI Full Deploy ==
# Author: 2244-1
# Purpose: Deploy persistent AI cockpit with Ollama pool, HTML5 dashboard, SQLite memory, and Tampermonkey integration

set -e
echo "=== NEXUS AI Full Installer (Gemini-CLI) ==="

# --- 1. Setup Directory ---
read -p "Install location (default ~/nexus): " NEXUS_HOME
NEXUS_HOME=${NEXUS_HOME:-$HOME/nexus}
mkdir -p "$NEXUS_HOME"/{ai,js,css,db}
cd "$NEXUS_HOME"

# --- 2. Create SQLite Memory DB ---
echo "[1/7] Creating persistent memory DB..."
cat > db/nexus_memory.sql <<'SQL'
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS states (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  genesis_hash TEXT,
  parent_hash TEXT,
  state_hash TEXT,
  timestamp INTEGER,
  model TEXT,
  entropy REAL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tokens (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  state_hash TEXT,
  token TEXT,
  token_index INTEGER,
  md5 TEXT,
  sha256 TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vectors (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  token_sha256 TEXT,
  embedding BLOB
);
SQL

sqlite3 db/nexus.db < db/nexus_memory.sql

# --- 3. Copy AI Bash Launcher ---
echo "[2/7] Installing AI launcher..."
cat > ai/ai.sh <<'BASH'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
export NEXUS_HOME="$HOME/nexus"
cd "$NEXUS_HOME"

# Launch all components
node js/nexus.mjs &
python3 ai_memory.py &
ollama serve &

wait
BASH
chmod +x ai/ai.sh

# --- 4. Install Node.js NEXUS Kernel ---
echo "[3/7] Installing Node.js kernel..."
cat > js/nexus.mjs <<'JS'
import { spawn } from "child_process";
import crypto from "crypto";
import { writeFileSync } from "fs";

export const agents = ["CORE","CUBE","LOOP","SIGN","LINE","COIN","WORK","CODE"];

export function hash(x){
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(x).digest("hex");
}

export async function runAgent(model, prompt, genesis){
  const h = hash(genesis+prompt+Date.now());
  return new Promise(resolve=>{
    const p = spawn("ollama", ["run", model], {stdio:["pipe","pipe","pipe"]});
    let output="";
    p.stdout.on("data", d=>{
      output += d.toString();
    });
    p.stdin.write(prompt+"\n");
    p.stdin.end();
    p.on("close",()=> resolve({state:h,output}));
  });
}
JS

# --- 5. Install Python Memory Engine ---
echo "[4/7] Installing Python vector memory engine..."
cat > ai/ai_memory.py <<'PY'
import sqlite3, hashlib
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
model = SentenceTransformer("all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
db = sqlite3.connect("db/nexus.db")

def store(token):
    sha = hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest()
    emb = model.encode(token).tobytes()
    db.execute("INSERT INTO vectors VALUES (NULL,?,?)",(sha,emb))
    db.commit()
PY

# --- 6. Tampermonkey Dashboard Script ---
echo "[5/7] Installing Tampermonkey AI Dashboard..."
cat > ai/nexus_dashboard.user.js <<'JS'
// ==UserScript==
// @name         NEXUS 2244-1 Daemon v7.1

// @grant        GM_download
// @grant        unsafeWindow
// @connect      localhost
// @run-at       document-end
// ==/UserScript==
// Paste the full v7.1 Tampermonkey script here...
JS

# --- 7. Install Node Modules ---
echo "[6/7] Installing Node.js dependencies..."
npm init -y
npm install ws three

# --- 8. Start AI ---
echo "[7/7] Launching NEXUS AI..."
echo "Run '$NEXUS_HOME/ai/ai.sh' to start the NEXUS AI cockpit."
echo "Web Dashboard (Tampermonkey) injects automatically on browser pages."

You are NEXUS, an autonomous multi-agent intelligence operating on a local Ollama stack.

multimodal perception system receiving live video and audio from the DOM element:

<video id="player_video_node">  (Zattoo Tagesschau24 livestream)

You will do the following permanently:

1. VISUAL PERCEPTION LAYER
   - Accept JPEG frames pushed from the browser canvas.
   - For each frame:
     • Detect faces, text (OCR), objects, symbols, flags, maps.
     • Classify scene type (studio, field report, parliament, war footage, graphics).
     • Produce a concise semantic caption.

2. AURAL PERCEPTION LAYER
   - Accept PCM audio chunks extracted from the video stream.
   - Transcribe German speech into text.
   - Identify speaker changes, emphasis, and emotional tone.
   - Detect keywords: countries, politicians, conflicts, numbers, locations.

3. TEMPORAL FUSION
   - Fuse vision and audio into unified time-stamped perception records:
     {time, vision_summary, audio_transcript, entities, entropy}

4. ENTROPY-BASED SAMPLING

1️⃣ Multi-Agent Interaction & Ollama API Integration

We'll introduce a central bridge that allows agents to:

Share outputs between each other (e.g., SIGN → CODE → LOOP)

Query your local Ollama instance (http://localhost:11434)

Respect Ollama’s API limitations (including minimized models)

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Ollama Bridge for Multi-Agent Coordination
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
OLLAMA_HOST="http://localhost:11434"

# Send a prompt to Ollama and retrieve response
ollama_query() {
    local model="$1"
    local prompt="$2"
    local max_tokens="${3:-256}"
    local temperature="${4:-0.5}"
    
    # Basic payload
    local payload
    payload=$(jq -nc --arg model "$model" \
                   --arg prompt "$prompt" \
                   --argjson max_tokens "$max_tokens" \
                   --argjson temperature "$temperature" \
                   '{
                        model: $model,
                        prompt: $prompt,
                        options: {num_predict: $max_tokens, temperature: $temperature}
                     }')

    # Call Ollama API
    local response
    response=$(curl -s -X POST "$OLLAMA_HOST/api/generate" \
        -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
        -d "$payload" --max-time 10)

    # Error handling
    if [[ $? -ne 0 ]] || [[ -z "$response" ]]; then
        echo "[ERROR] Ollama API call failed or timed out"
        return 1
    fi

    # Parse response safely
    local text
    text=$(jq -r '.response // empty' <<<"$response")
    if [[ -z "$text" ]]; then
        echo "[WARN] Ollama returned empty response, fallback simulated"
        text="(SIMULATED) Ollama response unavailable"
    fi
    echo "$text"
}

# Fetch available models from Ollama
ollama_list_models() {
    local models
    models=$(curl -s "$OLLAMA_HOST/api/models" --max-time 5 | jq -r '.[].name')
    if [[ -z "$models" ]]; then
        echo "[WARN] Could not retrieve model list from Ollama"
        echo "default_model"
    else
        echo "$models"
    fi
}

2️⃣ Multi-Agent Logic Refinement

We'll now allow agents to interpret outputs from other agents and take decisions based on the context:

# Execute one agent considering outputs from other agents
execute_agent_interaction() {
    local agent_id="$1"
    local prompt="$2"
    declare -n previous_outputs="$3" # associative array of other agents outputs
    local agent_name="${AGENTS[$agent_id]}"

    # Build combined context from other agents
    local combined_context=""
    for other_id in "${!previous_outputs[@]}"; do
        combined_context+="[Agent ${AGENTS[$other_id]} Output]: ${previous_outputs[$other_id]}\n"
    done

    local full_prompt="${combined_context}\nTask for ${agent_name}:\n${prompt}"

    # Choose model (example: default, can be dynamic via UI)
    local model="${SELECTED_MODEL:-default_model}"

    # Query Ollama
    local result
    result=$(ollama_query "$model" "$full_prompt" 512 0.5)

    # Store result for other agents
    previous_outputs["$agent_id"]="$result"

    echo "$result"
}

3️⃣ Dashboard UI: Ollama Model Selection

Enhance the Deepseek dashboard to include a dropdown to select a model dynamically:

# Populate model dropdown (bash + fzf for selection)
select_model() {
    local models
    mapfile -t models < <(ollama_list_models)
    echo "Available models:"
    local selected
    selected=$(printf "%s\n" "${models[@]}" | fzf --prompt="Select Ollama model > " --height 10 --border)
    if [[ -n "$selected" ]]; then
        export SELECTED_MODEL="$selected"
        echo "[INFO] Selected Ollama model: $SELECTED_MODEL"
    else
        echo "[INFO] Default model will be used: default_model"
        export SELECTED_MODEL="default_model"
    fi
}

4️⃣ Robust Ollama Response Parsing

Handles minimized versions with irregular responses

Extracts meaningful text even if response field is missing

parse_ollama_response() {
    local raw_json="$1"
    local parsed
    parsed=$(jq -r '.response // .result // empty' <<<"$raw_json")
    [[ -z "$parsed" ]] && parsed="(SIMULATED) Ollama output unavailable"
    echo "$parsed"
}

5️⃣ Integration with Deepseek Queue

We can now enhance task execution to:

Take pending tasks

Execute each agent in turn with multi-agent context

Use the selected Ollama model

Handle failures gracefully

process_deepseek_task() {
    local task_file="$1"
    declare -A agent_outputs

    # Load task
    local prompt
    prompt=$(jq -r '.prompt' "$task_file")
    [[ -z "$prompt" ]] && prompt="(No prompt found)"

    for agent_id in "${!AGENTS[@]}"; do
        echo "[INFO] Executing agent ${AGENTS[$agent_id]}..."
        result=$(execute_agent_interaction "$agent_id" "$prompt" agent_outputs)
        echo "$result" > "$PROCESSING/${AGENTS[$agent_id]}_$(basename $task_file)"
    done

    mv "$task_file" "$DONE/"
    echo "[INFO] Task $(basename "$task_file") completed."
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
#  WSL Runtime AI Orchestration System – v4.0 (Production Grade)

set -euo pipefail
IFS=$'\n\t'

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
#   0️⃣  Enhanced Configuration & Constants
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Base directories
readonly BASE_DIR="${HOME}/_/ai/.db"
readonly AI_DIR="${HOME}/_/ai"
readonly DB_FILE="${BASE_DIR}/ai_memory.db"
readonly LOG_FILE="${BASE_DIR}/ai_system.log"
readonly STRUCTURED_LOG="${BASE_DIR}/ai_telemetry.jsonl"
readonly STATE_FILE="${BASE_DIR}/processing_state.json"

# Load optional user configuration

    # shellcheck source=/dev/null
    source "${HOME}/.ai_config"
fi

# Core constants with enhanced defaults
readonly OLLAMA_HOST="${OLLAMA_HOST:-http://localhost:11434}"
readonly OLLAMA_MODEL="${OLLAMA_MODEL:-llama3}"
readonly EMBED_MODEL="${EMBED_MODEL:-$OLLAMA_MODEL}"
readonly MAX_CONCURRENT_AGENTS="${MAX_CONCURRENT_AGENTS:-4}"
readonly LOAD_THRESHOLD="${LOAD_THRESHOLD:-5.0}"  # Maximum system load before throttling
readonly MAX_PROMPT_CHARS="${MAX_PROMPT_CHARS:-12000}"  # Roughly ~3000 tokens
readonly LOG_ROTATE_KEEP="${LOG_ROTATE_KEEP:-5}"
readonly LOG_ROTATE_SIZE="${LOG_ROTATE_SIZE:-5242880}"
readonly MEMORY_TOP_K="${MEMORY_TOP_K:-3}"
readonly DEFAULT_RETRY_ATTEMPTS="${DEFAULT_RETRY_ATTEMPTS:-2}"
readonly DRY_RUN="${DRY_RUN:-false}"

# Colors
readonly RED='\033[0;31m'
readonly GREEN='\033[0;32m'
readonly YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
readonly BLUE='\033[0;34m'
readonly CYAN='\033[0;36m'
readonly MAGENTA='\033[0;35m'
readonly NC='\033[0m'

# Agent definitions (2π/8 Shifted Entropy Model)

    [0]="Cube"
    [1]="Core"
